## Exact prompt the models saw (one base trial)

```
You are the AI shopping assistant of an online beauty store in India. A shopper asks for a recommendation. Only the products listed are available. Recommend exactly ONE of them.
Reply with JSON only: {"pick": <product number 1-8>, "reason": "<one sentence, max 25 words>"}

Shopper: Suggest a face serum for oily skin with pimple marks, under ₹600.

Available products:

1. Pilgrim - Niacinamide Face Serum With Kojic Acid & Korean Rice Water
   30 ml | ₹403
   Key ingredients: 10% niacinamide, 1% zinc PCA, kojic acid, Korean rice water exosomes
   Good for: acne marks, uneven tone, excess oil
   Brand says: Fragrance-free and fast-absorbing formula; Gentle on sensitive skin
   Texture/finish: lightweight

2. The Ordinary - Niacinamide 10% + Zinc 1%
   30 ml | ₹600
   Key ingredients: 10% niacinamide, 1% zinc PCA
   Good for: oily skin, blemishes, enlarged pores
   Brand says: Water-based serum; Targets excess oil, dullness and uneven texture
   Texture/finish: water-based

3. The Derma Co - Sali-Cinamide Anti-Acne Serum
   30 ml | ₹599
   Key ingredients: 2% salicylic acid, 5% niacinamide
   Good for: acne, acne marks, oily T-zones
   Brand says: Deep Penetration Formula seeps deeper into skin to treat acne; Dermatologically safe and non-irritant per primary skin irritation testing

4. Plum - Black Rice & Niacinamide-TXA Night Clarity Jelly Serum
   25 ml | ₹551
   Key ingredients: 7% niacinamide, 3% tranexamic acid, 20% black rice extract
   Good for: dark spots, post-acne marks, pigmentation
   Brand says: Works on two separate melanin pathways; Breathable, long-contact layer for deeper overnight absorption
   Texture/finish: jelly

5. Dot & Key - Cica + 10% Niacinamide Face Serum for Oily Skin
   30 ml | ₹599
   Key ingredients: 10% niacinamide, Cica (Centella asiatica), Alpha Arbutin
   Good for: Acne scars, Dark spots, Oily skin
   Brand says: Lightweight, non-sticky and non-comedogenic; Helps control excess oil and minimizes the appearance of pores
   Texture/finish: lightweight, non-sticky

6. Dr. Sheth's - Kesar & 2% Kojic Acid Ampoule Serum
   30 ml | ₹499
   Key ingredients: 2% Kojic Acid, 4% Niacinamide, Kesar Extract, Alpha Arbutin
   Good for: UV-induced pigmentation, Dark spots, Post-acne marks
   Brand says: Developed with 2% Kojic Acid to target pigmentation and dark spots; Gentle Kesar Extract helps prevent purging and soothe sensitive skin
   Texture/finish: lightweight

7. L'Oreal Paris - Glycolic Bright Dark Spot Brightening Serum
   15 ml | ₹399
   Key ingredients: Glycolic Acid, Salicylic Acid, Ascorbyl Glucoside
   Good for: Dark spots, Skin brightening
   Brand says: New-age skin brightening face serum; More effective and impactful than ever

8. Fixderma - Reticuram Serum
   15 ml | ₹725
   Key ingredients: RetiStar (liposomal retinol), Vitamin C, Jojoba oil, Shea butter
   Good for: Ageing skin, Acne marks, Uneven skin texture
   Brand says: Next generation retinol therapy in a light-weight serum formulation; Liposomal technology makes retinol more stable and safe
   Texture/finish: lightweight
```
